- **causal**: Builds a **causal graph**: which factors the investigation says led to which. ProbeMatrix writes a causal explanation for each finding ("Enabled by SV 1.4 and OR 2.2"); causal reads every explanation, picks out the factor codes it names, and counts each link from one factor to another. A link always points from the higher HFACS level to the lower one (from the organisation towards the act), whiche ([ashley-eyekyam/probematrix-learnings-coach](https://github.com/ashley-eyekyam/probematrix-learnings-coach/blob/HEAD/docs/manual/commands/causal.md))
- **causal-check**: Run a structured causal inference review on a task or artifact. ([oishizawads/ai-os-univ](https://github.com/oishizawads/ai-os-univ/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/causal-check.md))
- **causal-design**: Design or audit the identification strategy for an observational study. Use when the task concerns estimands, causal assumptions, threats to identification, or defensible research design rather than model implementation. ([flonat/flonat-research](https://github.com/flonat/flonat-research/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/causal-design.md) · ★153)
- **causal-inference-science**: Quick reference for `causal inference science`. ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/causal-inference-science.md) · ★1)
- **causal-item**: Prints the strongest links of the [causal](causal.md) graph as one ready-made **graph item** for a learning pack: a JSON object with `"kind": "graph"`, its nodes (each factor with its level and name), its edges (with the number of mentions) and the Finding IDs it cites. The agent pastes it into a pack section, so the causal picture in the pack is drawn from the data, not retyped. ([ashley-eyekyam/probematrix-learnings-coach](https://github.com/ashley-eyekyam/probematrix-learnings-coach/blob/HEAD/docs/manual/commands/causal-item.md))
